# Israeli registered medicines list (from Medicines.xlsx)

Compact text version of the spreadsheet `Medicines.xlsx` (Israeli drug registry export, file dated 2026-10-07): 4914 human-use products (4781 distinct lines), sorted by English brand name. Veterinary products (392) were left out. Columns dropped to fit the project's space: dosage form, route, registration number and date, manufacturer, registration holder, and the indications text. Check the original spreadsheet for those.

Format per line: English brand | Hebrew brand | active ingredient(s) and strength | in health basket (סל): Y/N
